  var timer=0;

  function q(v){return String(v==null?'':v).trim()}
  function low(v){return q(v).toLowerCase()}

  function ensureStyle(){
    if(document.getElementById('rc888ShipmentCreateStyle')) return;
    var s=document.createElement('style');
    s.id='rc888ShipmentCreateStyle';
    s.textContent=atob(css);
    (document.head||document.documentElement).appendChild(s)
  }

  function stateObj(){
    try{
      if(typeof window.state==='function'){
        var s=window.state();
        if(s&&typeof s==='object') return s
      }
    }catch(_ ){}
    return window.STATE&&typeof window.STATE==='object'?window.STATE:{}
  }

  function currentView(){
    var s=stateObj();
    return low(
      s.view||
      (document.body?document.body.getAttribute('data-exporthub-view'):'')||
      (document.body?document.body.getAttribute('data-current-view'):'')
    )
  }

  function shell(){
    return document.getElementById('rc573ShipmentShell')||
           document.getElementById('rc363FixedShipmentLayout')
  }

  function isCreate(){
    var v=currentView();
    if(/create|shipmentcreate|newshipment|sendung.*erstell/.test(v)) return true;
    var sh=shell();
    if(!sh) return false;
    var h=sh.querySelector('h1,h2,h3');
    return !!(h&&/sendung.*erstell/i.test(q(h.textContent)))
  }

  var steps=[
    {id:'rc363BlockCustomer',n:'1',label:'Kunde & Empfänger'},
    {id:'rc363BlockShipment',n:'2',label:'Sendungsdaten'},
    {id:'rc573ColliCard',n:'3',label:'Colli & LDM'},
    {id:'rc363BlockDocuments',n:'4',label:'Dokumente'},
    {id:'rc363BlockStow',n:'5',label:'Stauplan'},
    {id:'rc363BlockAbdDecision',n:'6',label:'ABD'},
    {id:'rc363BlockMail',n:'7',label:'Mail'},
    {id:'rc363BlockActions',n:'8',label:'Speichern & Ausgabe'}
  ];

  function hasValue(el){
    if(!el) return false;
    if(el.type==='checkbox'||el.type==='radio') return !!el.checked;
    return !!q(el.value)
  }

  function blockReady(id){
    var el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(!el) return false;

    if(id==='rc363BlockStow'||id==='rc363BlockActions') return true;

    var fields=Array.prototype.slice.call(
      el.querySelectorAll('input:not([type="hidden"]),select,textarea')
    ).filter(function(x){return !x.disabled&&x.offsetParent!==null});

    if(!fields.length) return true;

    if(id==='rc573ColliCard'){
      var selects=fields.filter(function(x){return x.tagName==='SELECT'});
      var nums=fields.filter(function(x){return x.tagName==='INPUT'&&/number|text/.test(x.type)});
      return selects.some(hasValue)&&nums.some(hasValue)
    }

    return fields.some(hasValue)
  }

  function decorateBlocks(){
    steps.forEach(function(st){
      var el=document.getElementById(st.id);
      if(!el) return;

      el.setAttribute('data-rc888-step',st.n);

      var heading=el.querySelector('h1,h2,h3,h4,.title,.section-title');
      if(heading&&!heading.querySelector('.rc888-step-label')){
        var badge=document.createElement('span');
        badge.className='rc888-step-label';
        badge.textContent=st.n;
        heading.insertBefore(badge,heading.firstChild)
      }
    })
  }

  function countReady(){
    var total=0,ready=0;
    steps.forEach(function(st){
      if(document.getElementById(st.id)){
        total++;
        if(blockReady(st.id)) ready++
      }
    });
    return {ready:ready,total:total||steps.length}
  }

  function makeHeader(){
    var sh=shell();
    if(!sh) return null;

    var header=document.getElementById('rc888ShipmentHeader');
    if(!header){
      header=document.createElement('section');
      header.id='rc888ShipmentHeader';
      if(sh.firstElementChild) sh.insertBefore(header,sh.firstElementChild);
      else sh.appendChild(header)
    }

    var p=countReady();
    var percent=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round((p.ready/p.total)*100)));

    header.innerHTML=
      '<div class="rc888-top">'+
        '<div>'+
          '<div class="rc888-kicker">Sendung erstellen</div>'+
          '<div class="rc888-title">Versandauftrag</div>'+
          '<div class="rc888-sub">Alle Versanddaten in einem klaren Arbeitsablauf. Bestehende Funktionen und Speicherlogik bleiben unverändert.</div>'+
        '</div>'+
        '<div class="rc888-progress">'+
          '<div class="rc888-progress-text"><span>Bearbeitungsstand</span><strong>'+p.ready+' / '+p.total+'</strong></div>'+
          '<div class="rc888-progress-line"><div class="rc888-progress-fill" style="width:'+percent+'%"></div></div>'+
        '</div>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="rc888-steps">'+
        steps.filter(function(st){return !!document.getElementById(st.id)}).map(function(st){
          return '<button type="button" class="rc888-step" data-rc888-target="'+st.id+'" data-ready="'+(blockReady(st.id)?'1':'0')+'">'+
            st.n+' · '+st.label+
          '</button>'
        }).join('')+
      '</div>';

    return header
  }

  function removeUnwantedCreateControls(){
    var sh=shell();
    if(!sh) return;

    Array.prototype.slice.call(sh.querySelectorAll('button,a,[role="button"]')).forEach(function(el){
      var t=low(el.textContent);
      if(t==='kunde übernehmen'||t==='adresse geprüft und bestätigt'||t==='adresse prüfen'){
        el.style.display='none'
      }
    });

    // KundenAvis/Lieferavis belongs only to the dedicated Avis page.
    Array.prototype.slice.call(sh.querySelectorAll('*')).forEach(function(el){
      if(el.children.length>20) return;
      var t=low(el.textContent);
      if((t==='kunden-avis / lieferavis'||t==='kundenavis / lieferavis')&&
         !/avis/.test(currentView())){
        var card=el.closest('.card,.section,article,[id^="rc363Block"],[id*="Avis"]');
        if(card) card.style.display='none'
      }
    })
  }

  function markRequired(){
    var sh=shell();
    if(!sh) return;
    Array.prototype.slice.call(sh.querySelectorAll('[required]')).forEach(function(el){
      if(!hasValue(el)) el.classList.add('rc888-required');
      else el.classList.remove('rc888-required')
    })
  }

  function refresh(){
    timer=0;
    if(!isCreate()){
